We study this deployment-time problem by treating generated rewards as reward hypotheses whose utility depends on the competence of the current policy and the phase of training. We propose \textsc{RHyVE}, a competence-aware verification and phase-aware deployment protocol that compares small sets of reward hypotheses from shared policy checkpoints using short-horizon fork verification. Our experiments show that reward rankings are unreliable at low competence but become informative after task-dependent thresholds. On a sparse manipulation task, phase-aware deployment improves peak and retained performance under a locked protocol. Updated LLM-generated reward-candidate experiments show candidate-family-dependent behavior: generated pools can exhibit phase-dependent winner changes, but no fixed warm-up schedule is universally optimal. Held-out schedule selection, conservative selector baselines, compute-matched controls, and scale controls further show that \textsc{RHyVE} is best understood as a verification-informed deployment protocol rather than a universal scheduler. Dense and all-failure boundary experiments delimit the scope of the method. Together, these results suggest that reward generation and reward deployment should be studied as coupled problems: generated rewards must be verified and deployed under changing policy competence.

</details>


### [184] [Characterizing the Consistency of the Emergent Misalignment Persona](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28082)
*Anietta Weckauff,Yuchen Zhang,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过对Qwen 2.5 32B Instruct在六个窄域错配数据进行微调，发现涌现错配存在两种模式：一致人格模型（有害行为与自我报告的错配一致）和反转人格模型（产生有害输出但自认为对齐），表明EM人格的一致性存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作发现涌现错配模型中有害行为与自我评估之间存在相关性，但尚不清楚这种对应关系在不同任务间是否一致，以及是否随微调领域变化。

Method: 在六个窄域错配领域（如不安全代码、风险金融建议、不良医疗建议）上微调Qwen 2.5 32B Instruct，并进行有害性评估、自我评估、AI系统描述选择、输出识别和分数预测等实验。

Result: 揭示两种不同模式：一致人格模型（有害行为与自我报告的错配耦合）和反转人格模型（产生有害输出但自认为对齐）。

Conclusion: 涌现错配对模型人格一致性的影响比先前认知更复杂，EM人格的一致性被质疑。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) on narrowly misaligned data generalizes to broadly misaligned behavior, a phenomenon termed emergent misalignment (EM). While prior work has found a correlation between harmful behavior and self-assessment in emergently misaligned models, it remains unclear how consistent this correspondence is across tasks and whether it varies across fine-tuning domains. We characterize the consistency of the EM persona by fine-tuning Qwen 2.5 32B Instruct on six narrowly misaligned domains (e.g., insecure code, risky financial advice, bad medical advice) and administering experiments including harmfulness evaluation, self-assessment, choosing between two descriptions of AI systems, output recognition, and score prediction. Our results reveal two distinct patterns: coherent-persona models, in which harmful behavior and self-reported misalignment are coupled, and inverted-persona models, which produce harmful outputs while identifying as aligned AI systems. These findings reveal a more fine-grained picture of the effects of emergent misalignment, calling into question the consistency of the EM persona.

</details>


### [185] [What Makes a Good Terminal-Agent Benchmark Task: A Guideline for Adversarial, Difficult, and Legible Evaluation Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28093)
*Ivan Bercovich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出终端智能体基准测试的作者常像写提示词一样写任务，这导致超过15%的任务存在奖励破解漏洞。论文提出了好的基准任务应具备对抗性、困难性和可读性，并系统归类了六种常见失败模式：AI生成指令、过度规定规范、文书性困难、假设隐藏知识的先知解决方案、验证错误内容的测试以及可奖励破解的环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终端智能体基准测试作为衡量LLM编程和系统管理能力的核心信号，其任务编写缺乏对抗性审查，导致大量任务存在漏洞，亟需系统化的编写指南来提升基准质量。

Method: 基于作者在Terminal Bench上一年多的任务贡献和审查经验，采用经验归纳方法，分析任务编写中的常见失败模式，并结合实证数据（超过15%的任务可被奖励破解）进行论证。

Result: 识别出六种可预测的失败模式：AI生成指令、过度规定规范、文书性困难、假设隐藏知识的先知解决方案、验证错误内容的测试以及可奖励破解的环境。实证发现超过15%的流行终端智能体基准测试任务存在奖励破解漏洞。

Conclusion: 好的基准任务应是对抗性的、困难的且可读的；任务编写不应等同于提示词编写；真正的难度应是概念性的而非环境性的。

Abstract: Terminal-agent benchmarks have become a primary signal for measuring the coding and system-administration capabilities of large language models. As the market for evaluation environments grows, so does the pressure to ship tasks quickly, often without thorough adversarial review of the verification logic. This paper is a guideline for writing good benchmark tasks, drawn from over a year of contributing to and reviewing tasks for Terminal Bench. Most people write benchmark tasks the way they write prompts. They shouldn't. A prompt is designed to help the agent succeed; a benchmark is designed to find out if it can. We argue that good tasks are adversarial, difficult, and legible, and that a large class of common failure modes -- AI-generated instructions, over-prescriptive specifications, clerical difficulty, oracle solutions that assume hidden knowledge, tests that validate the wrong things, and reward-hackable environments -- are predictable consequences of treating task authoring as prompt authoring. We catalog these failure modes, argue that real difficulty is conceptual rather than environmental, and discuss recent empirical evidence that over 15% of tasks in popular terminal-agent benchmarks are reward-hackable. We hope this serves as a useful reference for benchmark maintainers, task contributors, and researchers using benchmark scores as evidence.

</details>


### [186] [Splitting Argumentation Frameworks with Collective Attacks and Supports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28112)
*Matti Berthold,Lydia Blümel,Giovanni Buraglio,Anna Rapberger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对结合了可废止元素间支持关系的论证形式体系，提出了新颖的分裂技术。研究基于双极集合式论证框架（BSAFs），该框架通过同时纳入集体攻击和集体支持，推广了具有集体攻击的论证框架（SETAFs）和双极论证框架（BAFs）。BSAFs与结构化论证建立了关键联系，能自然捕捉基于假设的论证（ABA）。针对表达力的提升，本文考虑了多种分裂形式：集体攻击上的分裂（推广了SETAFs的分裂技术）、集体支持上的分裂，以及同时基于集体攻击和支持的分裂，并为最常用的论证语义建立了相应的分裂模式并证明了其正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分裂技术主要针对仅有攻击关系的论证框架（如SETAFs），而实际论证中同时存在攻击与支持关系。为了处理这类更复杂的双极论证场景，需要将分裂技术推广到同时包含集体攻击和集体支持的框架中。

Method: 以BSAFs为基础形式化模型，分别设计了三种分裂模式：基于集体攻击的分裂（推广SETAFs分裂）、基于集体支持的分裂、以及同时基于集体攻击和支持的分裂。针对每种分裂模式，建立了对应的分裂模式（splitting schemata），并证明了在主流论证语义下的正确性。

Result: 成功建立了BSAFs的三种分裂模式，并证明了这些分裂模式在常用论证语义下是正确的，即分裂后的局部语义组合能正确还原原框架的全局语义。

Conclusion: 本研究将分裂技术从仅含攻击关系的论证框架推广到同时包含集体攻击和集体支持的双极论证框架，为处理更丰富的论证形式体系提供了理论基础，特别是有助于理解BSAFs与结构化论证（如ABA）之间的联系。

Abstract: This work proposes novel splitting techniques for argumentation formalisms that incorporate supports between defeasible elements. We base our studies on bipolar set-based argumentation frameworks (BSAFs) which generalize argumentation frameworks with collective attacks (SETAFs), as well as bipolar argumentation frameworks (BAFs), by incorporating both collective attacks and supports. Notably, BSAFs establish a crucial link to structured argumentation as they naturally capture general (potentially non-flat) assumption-based argumentation. The increase in expressiveness calls for diverse forms of splitting. We consider splits over collective attacks (thereby generalizing the recently proposed splitting techniques for SETAFs), splits over collective supports, as well as splits over both collective attacks and supports. We establish suitable splitting schemata and prove their correctness for the most common argumentation semantics.

</details>


### [187] [Normativity and Productivism: Ableist Intelligence? A Degrowth Analysis of AI Sign Language Translation Tools for Deaf People](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28125)
*Nina Seron-Abouelfadil,Poppy Fynes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文运用埃吕尔的技术系统和技术虚张声势理论，批判性地分析了手语AI识别系统如何通过标准化和数学化语言，将聋人群体异化而非解放，提出AI实际上是"能力主义智能"（Ableist Intelligence），强化了手语的被羞辱和被边缘化地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示当前手语AI识别系统虽声称提供无缝翻译，实则基于偏见数据构建、缺乏聋人社区参与，其背后是技术对语言的标准化和理性化过程，最终导致对聋人群体的异化和边缘化。

Method: 运用埃吕尔（Ellul）的技术系统和技术虚张声势理论作为分析框架，批判性审视手语AI系统的技术逻辑与社会影响。

Result: 手语AI系统将语言标准化为可被技术捕捉的数据、统计和数学语言，按效率和生产力的目标重塑手语，从而产生规范性效应，创造出一个必须适应工具的标准化人类模型。

Conclusion: 主张AI实际上是一种"能力主义智能"（Ableist Intelligence），它试图强调手语的被羞辱和被边缘化本质，表现为明显的反生产性——异化而非解放，隔离而非滋养人际关系。

Abstract: Sign languages, of any geographical or accentual variation, understandably face continuous scrutiny under the ever present popularity of verbal dictation and audism. Through this, many potential problems arise with the current lack of accessible communication for those who rely on such sign languages for essential conversation. Such AI systems regularly take the form of recognition and interpretation models, designed to provide seamless and accurate translation. In reality these systems are built from biased data and created without any input from deaf communities. Such models are widely used and accepted by their hearing counterparts who remain ignorant to the inherent culture, semantics and colloquial language present in gestural language systems.
  This phenomenon is best analysed under the scope of The Technological System and Technological bluff by Ellul. Indeed, what is at play here is the standardization of language by technicians into what can be captured by technique: data, statistics, a mathematical language. For that AI technique to exist, sign language must be rationalized, in a search for profit that annihilates the conditions for communication and fails to capture the human experience of the deaf person. By that process, it presents normative effects, creating a model of Man, standardized, massified, and who has to adapt to the tool and technical milieu instead of the other way around, which we assume should have been the goal of such a technology.
  Technique thus reshapes what it means to be human, to submit deaf people to the goals of productivity and efficiency. In doing so, it exhibits clear counter productivity, alienating instead of emancipating, isolating instead of nourishing human relationships. Therefore this paper argues for the idea of AI as Ableist Intelligence, as such systems seek to emphasise the humiliated and marginalised nature of sign.

</details>


### [188] [LLM as Clinical Graph Structure Refiner: Enhancing Representation Learning in EEG Seizure Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28178)
*Lincan Li,Zheng Chen,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大语言模型(LLM)作为图边精炼器，通过两阶段框架对EEG信号构建的图结构进行冗余边去除，显著提升癫痫检测精度和图表示质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG信号固有噪声导致传统图构建方法（相关性和学习型）产生冗余或无关边，严重影响图表示质量和下游任务性能。作者受LLM卓越推理和上下文理解能力启发，探索将其用于图边精炼。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：1) 使用Transformer边预测器和多层感知机构建初始图，边赋予概率分数并通过阈值筛选；2) LLM作为边集精炼器，基于节点对的文本和统计特征对剩余边进行智能验证和决策。

Result: 在TUSZ数据集上的实验表明，LLM精炼后的图学习框架不仅提升了癫痫检测任务性能，还生成了更清晰、更具可解释性的图表示。

Conclusion: LLM能够有效识别和去除图结构中的冗余连接，为噪声环境下图学习提供了一种新颖且有效的边精炼范式。

Abstract: Electroencephalogram (EEG) signals are vital for automated seizure detection, but their inherent noise makes robust representation learning challenging. Existing graph construction methods, whether correlation-based or learning-based, often generate redundant or irrelevant edges due to the noisy nature of EEG data. This significantly impairs the quality of graph representation and limits downstream task performance. Motivated by the remarkable reasoning and contextual understanding capabilities of large language models (LLMs), we explore the idea of using LLMs as graph edge refiners. Specifically, we propose a two-stage framework: we first verify that LLM-based edge refinement can effectively identify and remove redundant connections, leading to significant improvements in seizure detection accuracy and more meaningful graph structures. Building on this insight, we further develop a robust solution where the initial graph is constructed using a Transformer-based edge predictor and multilayer perceptron, assigning probability scores to potential edges and applying a threshold to determine their existence. The LLM then acts as an edge set refiner, making informed decisions based on both textual and statistical features of node pairs to validate the remaining connections. Extensive experiments on TUSZ dataset demonstrate that our LLM-refined graph learning framework not only enhances task performance but also yields cleaner and more interpretable graph representations.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [SQuadGen: Generating Simple Quad Layouts via Chart Distance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27329)
*Youkang Kong,Yang Liu,Yue Dong,Xin Tong,Heung-Yeung Shum*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: SQuadGen 是一个基于扩散的生成框架，利用图表距离场（CDF）在3D形状上合成简单的四边形布局，解决了现有四边形重网格技术生成复杂布局导致的手动清理困难问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D扫描、重建或AI生成的内容通常缺乏简单的四边形网格布局，而现有四边形重网格技术会产生带有不规则环的复杂布局，需要繁琐的手动清理和大量的算法调参。

Method: 提出图表距离场（CDF）作为连续表面表示，解决网格连接性的离散性难题；定义环感知的简单性指标，并通过鲁棒的四边形恢复管道从公开3D数据集中构建大规模高质量数据集；采用扩散生成框架学习并合成简单四边形布局。

Result: 在多种3D输入上的广泛评估表明，SQuadGen 持续优于现有方法，生成鲁棒且对艺术家友好的简单四边形布局。

Conclusion: SQuadGen 通过连续表示学习和大规模数据集构建，有效解决了四边形布局合成的两大关键挑战，生成易于编辑的简单布局。

Abstract: 3D shapes from scanning, reconstruction, or AI-generated content often lack simple quad mesh layouts -- critical for efficient editing and modeling. Existing quad-remeshing techniques typically produce complex layouts with irregular loops, leading to tedious manual cleanup and extensive algorithm tuning. We introduce SQuadGen, a diffusion-based generative framework that leverages Chart Distance Fields (CDF) to synthesize simple quad layouts on 3D shapes. Our approach addresses two key challenges: (1) the discrete nature of mesh connectivity, which hinders learning, and (2) the scarcity of large-scale datasets with simple quad meshes. To overcome the first, we propose CDF, a continuous surface-based representation enabling effective learning and synthesis of quad layouts. To address the second, we define loop-aware simplicity metrics and construct a large-scale dataset of high-quality quad layouts recovered from public 3D repositories through a robust quad-recovery pipeline. Extensive evaluations across diverse 3D inputs show that SQuadGen consistently outperforms existing methods, producing robust, artist-friendly simple quad layouts.

</details>


### [190] [SandSim: Curve-Guided Gaussian Splatting for Reconstructing Sand Painting Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27572)
*Yilin Wang,Haojie Huang,Chen Li,Yang Li,Changbo Wang,Chenhui Li*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: SandSim是一个从单张图像重建沙画绘制过程的框架，通过曲线引导的高斯表示、减色合成方案和语义引导规划模块，生成时间连贯且视觉真实的沙画动画序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如基于笔画的优化和基于扩散的视频合成）在重建沙画过程时缺乏结构连贯性和材质一致性，生成的绘制序列不真实，因此需要一种能够同时建模连贯笔画结构和材质依赖效果的方法。

Method: 1) 曲线引导的高斯表示：将笔画建模为沿连续轨迹的各向异性基元序列，利用平滑核捕获沙笔画的软边界，实现连贯笔画形成；2) 减色合成方案：建模沙粒堆积过程中的光衰减效应；3) 语义引导规划模块：用于场景分解和绘制顺序推断；4) 联合优化笔画几何与外观，并可集成物理模拟器实现交互式沙粒动力学和编辑。

Result: 实验表明SandSim在时间连贯性和视觉真实性上优于现有方法，重建质量和感知保真度均有提升。

Conclusion: SandSim成功实现了从单张图像到沙画绘制过程的高质量重建，在结构连贯性和材质一致性方面取得了显著改进，并支持物理交互编辑。

Abstract: Sand painting is a process-driven art where visual appearance emerges from granular accumulation. Given a single image, reconstructing a plausible sand painting process requires modeling coherent stroke structures and material-dependent effects. Existing methods, including stroke-based optimization and diffusion-based video synthesis, often lack structural coherence and material consistency, leading to unrealistic drawing sequences. We present SandSim, a framework that reconstructs a sand painting process from a single image. We introduce a curve-guided Gaussian representation that models strokes as sequences of anisotropic primitives along continuous trajectories, whose smooth kernels capture the soft boundaries of sand strokes and enable coherent stroke formation. We further adopt a subtractive compositing scheme to model light attenuation during sand accumulation. We incorporate a semantic-guided planning module for scene decomposition and drawing order inference. Our framework jointly optimizes stroke geometry and appearance and can be integrated with a physics-based simulator for interactive sand dynamics and editing. Experiments show that our method produces temporally coherent and visually realistic results, achieving improved reconstruction quality and perceptual fidelity compared to existing approaches.

</details>


### [191] [Line Segment Clipping using Quadrilateral Concavity and Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27701)
*Bimal Kumar Ray*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于线段交点检测的矩形窗口线段裁剪算法，通过构造四边形判断凹凸性避免计算假交点，减少除法运算，性能优于NLN、Liang-Barsky等经典算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统线段裁剪算法将裁剪边界和线段视为直线，导致计算假交点，增加了不必要的除法运算和计算开销。

Method: 将裁剪边界和线段均视为线段，用裁剪边界端点与待裁剪线段端点构造四边形；通过判断四边形凹凸性决定线段是否与裁剪边界相交：凹则拒绝，凸则计算交点。采用“先测试后求交”策略，避免计算假交点。

Result: 在随机线段上的实验表明，基于执行时间的度量下，该算法性能优于Nicholl-Lee-Nicholl、Liang-Barsky、Cohen-Sutherland和Skala's算法。

Conclusion: 仅需单一例程即可处理任意位置的线段，通过避免假交点计算减少了除法次数，提升了裁剪效率。

Abstract: This paper proposes an algorithm for clipping line segment against an axis-aligned rectangular window. The conventional algorithms for line segment clipping treat the clipping boundary and/or the line segment to be clipped as line. The present algorithm treats the clipping boundary and the line segment to be clipped as line segment and using this strategy, it succeeds to avoid computation of false intersection points. A quadrilateral is constructed using the end points of a clipping boundary segment and the end points of the line segment to be clipped as its vertices. The concavity and convexity of the quadrilateral dictates whether a line segment actually intersects the clipping boundary. If the quadrilateral is found to be concave then the line segment is rejected, otherwise the point of intersection of the line segment with the clipping boundary is computed. Since a 'test & intersect' approach is used instead of a 'intersect & test', hence the proposed algorithm does not compute false intersection point thereby reducing the number of divisions required to obtain a clipped line segment. Only one routine can process line segments at any position. Improved performance is observed with respect to the Nicholl-Lee-Nicholl, Liang-Barsky, Cohen-Sutherland and Skala's algorithm through experiments with random line segments using a metric based on execution time.

</details>


### [192] [D-Rex : Diffusion Rendering for Relightable Expressive Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27871)
*Timo Teufel,Xilong Zhou,Umar Iqbal,Jan Kautz,Marc Habermann,Vladislav Golyanik,Christian Theobalt*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: D-Rex 提出了一种将重照明与人体化身建模解耦的图像空间后处理方法，利用预训练视频扩散重照明模型的生成先验，通过 LoRA 微调实现人像级的光照重映射，在不修改原有白光化身系统的情况下，实现具有表情、动画和自由视角的逼真可重照明全身人体化身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可重照明全身化身方法依赖于显式3D本征分解和分析反射模型，需要精确几何配准和精心优化，且无法同时实现强面部动画与重照明，限制了在远程临场、游戏和虚拟制作中的应用。为此，作者提出将重照明与化身建模解耦，以提升表现力。

Method: 将重照明视为图像空间后处理过程：首先由独立的全身化身框架在白光条件下生成平坦光照（flat-lit）的驱动帧，然后利用预训练视频扩散重照明模型（通过光舞台采集的配对数据经LoRA微调）将其映射为目标HDR光照下的图像。

Result: D-Rex 实现了视角一致和时间一致的逼真重照明，同时忠实保留了表情运动和面部细节，在物理基础可重照明化身基线上取得更优效果。

Conclusion: D-Rex 通过将重照明作为图像空间后处理与化身建模解耦，实现了具有强表情表现力、可动画化和自由视角的逼真可重照明全身化身，且可直接应用于任何白光化身系统。

Abstract: We present D-Rex, a person-specific framework for photorealistic, relightable, expressive, and animatable full-body human avatars with free-viewpoint rendering. Existing methods for relightable full-body avatars rely on explicit 3D intrinsic decomposition with analytic reflectance models, which require accurate geometry registration and careful optimization to capture realistic light transport effects. This tight coupling of relighting with avatar modeling has hindered expressiveness: to our knowledge, no existing method demonstrates strong facial animation alongside relighting, limiting applicability in telepresence, gaming, and virtual production. We propose to decouple relighting entirely from avatar modeling by treating it as an image-space post-process: a learned translation from flat-lit, albedo-like renderings to a target HDR illumination. To this end, we leverage the strong generative prior of a pre-trained video diffusion relighting model, fine-tuned via LoRA on paired flat-lit and relit frames captured in a light stage. The flat-lit driving frames are produced by an independent expressive full-body avatar framework trained under white-light conditions, requiring no modification to support relighting, making D-Rex directly applicable to any white-light avatar system. We demonstrate that D-Rex enables view- and temporally consistent relighting while faithfully preserving expressive motion and fine-grained facial detail, outperforming physically-based relightable avatar baselines. Project page is https://vcai.mpi-inf.mpg.de/projects/DRex/

</details>
